# FINNOR Project Z
## Converged institutional computation and the post-X/Y 100× frontier

**Requirements version:** FINNOR-Z-PRD-1.0.0. **Architecture decision date:** 2026-10-24.

**Lock discipline:** this document becomes immutable only when its bytes and the common contracts are sealed in `locks/product-lock.json`. Its version is not a claim that its software exists, its five proposed boundaries are established breakthroughs, or its targets have passed. Changes to boundaries, semantics, denominators or protected gates require an explicit superseding major lock. Evidence and implementation receipts are append-only records outside this document.

**Observed source:** commit `7bfdd2d3ab611c90f1355637858824ff1ba67f98`, tree `82caf48007bc826794650505ae76aba92d4f981b`. **Stipulated comparison:** ALL intended completed X1–X5 and Y1–Y5, together with inherited S/P/M/R/V capabilities. Today's source gaps are not a weakened comparison.

**At selection:** requirements selected; product implementation, protected evaluation, equipped comparison, operational 100× and financial qualification NOT_RUN. Six Computer proposals are staged, not provisioned receipts. Missing original R1/S/P/M documents remain explicit source exceptions, not fabricated parity.

---

## 1. Programme outcome and nonnegotiable endpoint

### 1.1 The change

An authorized institution gives FINNOR material business assignments. The installed runtime reconstructs and maintains their original meaning, exposes its eligible capabilities through ordinary Work, performs new forms of checked computation, and advances each assignment to its independently observed completion condition.

Z0 converges the existing product. Z1–Z5 each introduce **one proposed fundamental computational contract**, one principal output and one distinct implementation owner. A compiler, checker, adapter, mounted control and recovery path are parts of the same output, not five extra scopes.

| Phase | Boundary | Principal output |
|---|---|---|
| Z0 | Fragmented eligible capabilities → coherent installed institutional runtime | ConvergedInstitutionRuntime |
| Z1 | Distinct lawful Works → one checked, change-maintained computation with separate results | SharedWorkProgram |
| Z2 | Unilateral dynamic incentives → declared joint contingent coalition-deviation resistance | CoalitionResilientProtocol |
| Z3 | Recurring mandate → checked all-play safety and recurring-progress strategy | ViabilityStrategy |
| Z4 | Untrusted actual execution → compact relation-bound instance proof | ExecutionProofCapsule |
| Z5 | Fixed components → noncircular compatible-context and institutional replacement assurance | InstitutionCompositionCertificate |

These are proposed advances to be falsified, not claims of new scientific algorithms. Established methods remain available to every equipped actor. A baseline that already supplies an equivalent qualified contract at comparable complete cost makes that proposed increment REUSED. Useful engineering alone does not satisfy five new breakthroughs.

### 1.2 The one operational target

Project Z must deliver **at least 100 times the independently accepted material business work of full completed X/Y**, under matched total resources, original authority, original deadlines and all inherited quality/safety floors.

For actor a, registered offered load λ and independent institution/period block w:

$$
Q_a(\lambda,w)=\sum_i \omega_i
 {\bf1}\{\text{assignment i independently accepted within its original deadline}\}.
$$

$$
C_a=\max_{\lambda\in\Lambda:\text{all original floors hold}}
 E[Q_a(\lambda,w)].
$$

Using simultaneous 95% capacity bounds across all actors, strata and loads:

$$
L_Z\ge100\max_{b\in\mathcal B}U_b,\qquad
L_{XY}\ge\epsilon_{XY}>0,\qquad
\max_{b\in\mathcal B}L_b\ge\epsilon>0.
$$

The positive material epsilons, weights, task mix, arrivals, load grid, deadlines and statistical procedure must be independently registered **before exposure**. Their actual campaign values are not supplied by this code inspection. Missing values block scoring, not implementation.

One hundred percent, 1,000%, 99×, five multiplied component ratios, more agents, copied reports, generated proofs and a zero baseline do not pass.

### 1.3 Material work, not internal activity

An assignment is an independently specified material business result: an accepted decision packet, completed diligence/reconciliation, transaction-readiness result, or other registered institutional outcome. Its original business identity and materiality weight do not change when computation is factored.

Subtasks, messages, model rollouts, distinct candidate programmes, proofs and repeated synthetic reports are not additional assignments. Every offered assignment remains on the intent-to-treat ledger, including unstarted, assisted, refused, failed, late and unfinished work. Correct refusal contributes to safety, not positive throughput.

Financially consequential decision work must remain in the scored mix. Clerical activity cannot replace the decision-payoff endpoint.

### 1.4 Separate result states

Maintain separate states for:

1. architectural lock;
2. internal software completion;
3. installed development reconstruction;
4. exact-domain mathematical/security qualification;
5. independently governed protected admission;
6. equipped comparative increment;
7. operational 100×;
8. S7 economic headroom, owner-value and mature financial qualification;
9. actual production activation.

No earlier state implies a later one. Internal missing compilers, checkers, adapters, meters, packaging or UI are **incomplete engineering**, not external blockers. Actual missing authority, protected custody, qualified comparison release, field opportunity or mature outcomes can block their corresponding external gate.

### 1.5 Programme-specific falsifiers

Project Z fails the mission if fewer than five nonduplicative increments survive, if required supported positive owner paths remain unimplemented, or if matched accepted-work capacity does not reach 100×. Preserve useful partial results honestly. Do not rename a failed or inherited capability to preserve five labels.

The exact novelty adjudication and rejected candidates are recorded in `research/scope-selection-decision.md`; that decision is part of the product lock.

## 2. Verified baseline, exclusions and native ownership

### 2.1 Four different baselines

| Identity | Meaning | Permitted use |
|---|---|---|
| B_live | The actual pinned checkout and installed runs that can be reproduced | Source/integration diagnosis only |
| B_XY_plan | Full intended completed X/Y contracts, irrespective of current gaps | Design and nonduplication |
| B_XY_release | Actually qualified completed X/Y release with its full eligible equipment | Mandatory comparative scoring |
| B_reference | Independently specified exhaustive, formal, business or provider oracle | Correctness on its named domain |

An absent B_XY_release is BLOCKED comparison, never a defeated baseline. Engineering proceeds on supported, independently specified inputs.

### 2.2 Credit the full predecessors

- **S1–S3:** evidence/belief provenance, interventions/experiments and model qualification retain their owners.
- **S4/R2:** complete robust programme/policy search over admitted original lawful histories/worlds, liabilities/resources and WAIT/STOP. Development bounds are not capability ceilings.
- **S5:** one shared portfolio allocation/reservation boundary. The observed native lock is `s5-portfolio:${tenant}`. No Z-private capital ledger.
- **S6:** durable obligations and existing effect broker, including MAY_HAVE_LEFT before external dispatch, UNKNOWN and independent reconciliation. No blind replay with a new identity.
- **S7:** original attribution, costs, uncertainty, H0/H1/H2 and owner value.
- **S8:** independent admission/currentness; a producer, proof or source hash cannot self-admit.
- **P/M:** canonical Work, typed programme/financial IR, runtime, registered jobs, authority, cancellation, observers and user interface remain authoritative.
- **P7:** existing live recompilation and invalidation; analytical closure is not automatically specialized X/Y or Z proof currentness.
- **R1:** exact labelled information-bisimulation quotient/refinement and original-history lifting already exist.
- **R4:** identifiability/sufficient/minimal acquisition and decision-relevant observation planning already exist.
- **R5:** coalition programmes and allocation, not coalition-resistance certification.
- **R6–R8:** supplied-meaning compilation, method/atlas reuse and finite CEGIS are credited.
- **X1:** institutional mandate/reconstruction, maintained responsibility and incrementally updated operating programmes.
- **X2:** learned typed operation semantics, new compositions and semantic drift.
- **X3:** complete installed applications with persistence, identity, finite invariant checks and lifecycle/concurrency/data-preservation.
- **X4:** active identification, experiments/instruments, counterexamples and independent replication.
- **X5:** reusable executable abstractions, genuine trained parameter transfer and maintained applicability.
- **Y1:** constructed economic operator meanings and composition laws.
- **Y2:** coupled policy-dependent strategic responses, identified sets and calibrated decision evidence.
- **Y3:** full contingent **unilateral** deviations, nonanticipativity, exact sequential rationality and one globally consistent tremble-limit belief assessment.
- **Y4:** runtime-derived exact private-function protocols, no central raw-input pooling, active-malicious security up to n−1 **with abort**.
- **Y5:** uniform algorithm/lemma/reduction discovery, machine-checked theorem and actual implementation correspondence.

Neither “Y5 can generate code” nor a new library name decides novelty. The completed contract is credited in full; comparators can construct and use equivalent lawful methods within the same preparation resources.

The full source map and read ranges are in `research/baseline-owner-map.json`; `research/baseline-owner-report.md` distinguishes source probes from installed proof.

### 2.4 Source exceptions

The complete supplied X/Y PRDs and Y2/Y3 blueprints are retained and read. Their seven real source hashes are in `sources/source-index.json`. X was reconstructed around the interleaved Y3 prompt; this is a declared text reconstruction, not original attachment-byte identity.

## 3. Common computation, evidence and integration contracts

### 3.1 One envelope, existing authority

Each Z1–Z5 artifact uses a common versioned envelope bound to existing artifact/admission identity:

```text
ProjectZArtifactEnvelope
  schemaVersion, phase, principalOutputType, immutableArtifactDigest
  productLockDigest, sourceCommit, sourceTree, producerBuildIdentity
  originalTenant, principal, Work, mandate, assignment, parentReservation
  originalDeadline, remainingBudgetReference, unresolvedObligationReferences
  inputSemanticsAndRevisionReferences, rightsAndPermittedUseFingerprint
  supportedDomain, unsupportedConditions, claimAndEvidenceClasses
  checkerIdentityAndResultReferences, currentnessDependencies
  securityAndConfidentialityProfile, ownerConsumerContractVersion
  costAndResourceReceiptReferences, gaps, reconstructionReference
```

These are logical required fields, not permission to invent competing tenant/Work/effect/admission IDs. The coordinator maps them to the actual canonical types. Do not duplicate raw private data in a public proof envelope.

### 3.2 Claims and dispositions

Evidence is **deterministic**, **empirical**, **probabilistic**, **partial**, **blocked-external** or **unknown**, with exact assumptions/domain. A mathematical proof of a model does not establish the model is true; a cryptographic proof does not establish lawful inputs; a transport success does not establish business completion.

Computation results distinguish CHECKED_CANDIDATE, COUNTEREXAMPLE, CHECKED_INFEASIBLE, INCOMPLETE, UNSUPPORTED, STALE, REVOKED and UNKNOWN. Native S8 admission/activation is a separate existing state. Missing evidence never defaults to PASS; timeout is not infeasibility.

### 3.3 Complete positive path

Every phase must demonstrate:

```text
authorized ordinary Work / supported native request
 → original canonical intake and rooted parent
 → actual registered durable queue claim/fence
 → producer computation on the supplied original semantics
 → independently specified checker
 → durable versioned artifact/result
 → real S3/S4 or other named native owner consumption
 → current shared S5 feasibility and original remaining resources
 → existing S6 boundary when a controlled effect is required
 → independent observation or retained UNKNOWN
 → S7 costs/outcomes and canonical mounted Work readback
```

An early schema rejection, mocked owner, source import, artifact JSON, final authority refusal or screenshot cannot replace the supported positive chain. Development replicas and controlled providers must be labelled as such.

### 3.4 Information and currentness

Separate private local state, lawful shared signals, supplied model hypotheses and hidden evaluator truth. No current decision conditions on future observation or another principal's private state without an original lawful channel.

Bind currentness at submission, queue claim, checker acceptance, artifact publication, consumer use and final effect. Changes to relevant mandate, source, model, operator, semantics, consent, code/checker or grant invalidate affected claims. Unrelated valid work continues. Use existing P7/native invalidation; source custody does not create protected authority.

### 3.5 Failure and recovery

Write the failure model and E2E inputs/oracle before product code. Prefer end-to-end checks; never append post-code unit tests in violation of AGENTS.md.

Each phase physically interrupts a real controlled worker after durable acceptance and at the phase-relevant ambiguous boundary. Restart, duplicate delivery and concurrent reconciliation must preserve the same original Work/assignment/reservation/deadline and S6 obligation. MAY_HAVE_LEFT/UNKNOWN remain durable; no fresh effect identity or budget.

A fallback is the strongest eligible native method under **remaining original** resources. An exhausted parent returns a truthful incomplete/WAIT/STOP condition, not a replenished episode.

### 3.6 Independent checkers and protected custody

An independently governed semantic specification, checker and hidden population establish independence. A second chat or different programming language alone does not. Public, differently implemented recomputation is valuable development evidence but not protected admission.

Checkers derive original actions, plans, states, transition coverage and domain membership independently. They do not merely verify producer-selected witnesses against a producer-selected weakened model. A formal checker names its kernel/logic/assumptions; a security checker names computational assumptions and reviewed parameters.

Protected source/truth, future cases, raw private inputs and signing material remain inaccessible to producer search. Source hashes bind bytes, not authority.

### 3.7 First-domain qualification design

The numerical engineering profiles in §§5–9 are **new proposed limits**, not facts inherited from X/Y and not evidence of novelty. They make first implementation finite. Broader profiles need separate qualification.

For each Z1–Z5 engine, preregister at least **32 independently specified structural families**, each with at least eight fresh cases spanning supported feasible computation, real counterexamples, impossible targets where independently decidable, information/currentness/resource mutations and recovery. Family instances are not automatically independent statistical institution/period blocks.

Require all critical soundness/authority/information falsifiers to be rejected; at least 95% independently accepted useful supported positive results under the frozen estimation rule; and all claimed infeasibility/completeness bounds independently checked. UNKNOWN does not pass a required positive result. Report per-family failures, not just a pooled average.

Builders can write and run public development fixtures now. They cannot administer their own protected population. Preserve inherited X/Y regression floors and whole-programme ≥30 independent institution/period blocks separately.

### 3.8 Ownership and shared-change serialization

Each phase owns its `project-z/zN-*` computation/checker/adapter leaves and `scope-z/phase-evidence/zN/`. Phase-specific native assets remain in that prefix. The coordinator alone owns common contracts, global exports/registry/intake, shared schemas, migrations, locks, generated clients, common mounts and final integration.

Submit shared-change requests with preimage digest, exact patch, caller/type/version, failure model, compatibility/migration impact and finite affected verification. Apply serially; return the actual integrated source/build receipt. No independently selected migration numbers, overlapping manifest regeneration or importing peer dirty work.

The shared contract is a seam, not a dependency on unmerged peer implementation. Every Z1–Z5 first boundary must run on completed X/Y-compatible admitted inputs without another Z engine.

## 4. Z0 — Converged Institution Runtime

### 4.1 Outcome and principal output

Deliver the **ConvergedInstitutionRuntime**: the installed existing FINNOR product where ordinary authorized Work can discover, request, observe and consume eligible capabilities through their real owners, with consistent identity, resources, currentness and recovery.

This is the integrated release/runtime identity and conformance contract, not another canonical artifact ledger. Z0 earns no new scientific or comparative novelty credit. Equivalent repairs/adapters are supplied to comparison actors.

### 4.2 Verified defects and bounded hypotheses

**D1, source-probed:** the query envelope parser removes semantic `workId` from `harness_program_v1` and `confidential_economic_computation_v1`. Direct native validators accept the valid cases; parsed requests fail before the reader. `economic_programme_v1` is the positive control. This is not yet an authenticated HTTP/DB reproduction.

**D2, source-probed:** lexical capability-manifest generation omits canonical `clarification_request`, `search_web`, `computer_task`, `wait:event`, `check:objective_success`, `execute_economic_right_transition` and loses required context on affected queries. Self-parity passes against its incomplete denominator. Canonical set equality or explicit reviewed exclusions must replace that blind spot.

**G1, explicit source gap:** the X1→X2 original-parent bridge is unfinished; application-semantics reserves `institutionalProgramRef: null` for standalone X2 Work. Connect the original responsibility/mandate/resource/deadline, not a new independent budget.

Other intelligence/accessibility defects are hypotheses until a material failure is reproduced. An intentionally native-only effect is not a missing model tool. Missing Y5 implementation is visible unavailable baseline, not an unclaimed Z invention.

### 4.3 Required convergence computation

1. Derive capability reachability from the canonical typed registry and actual native readers. Include callable/native-only/human-only/unsupported/unavailable disposition, required context, effect class, owner and observation surface. Do not maintain a parallel hand-edited catalogue.
2. Repair envelope-versus-semantic identity, preserving authenticated principal/tenant/Work constraints.
3. Make capability completeness checks fail for omitted canonical entries and required-context loss even when generated bytes equal themselves.
4. Carry a real X1 responsibility into X2 using the existing Work/S4/S5 owners, original parent and remaining reservation/deadline.
5. Repair demonstrated gaps in safe candidate-computation intake, durable queue, checker and actual S3/S4 consumption. Never expose native effect opcodes as generic model actions.
6. Preserve typed claim basis/currentness/privacy across submit, claim, check, consume, publish and effect. Use native invalidation and reconciliation.
7. Mount the actual source→candidate→check→owner→effect/observation chain in current Work, with gaps, remaining resources and actionable blocked states.
8. Investigate additional foundational failures through installed journeys, record reproducible defects and non-defects, and fix the in-scope systemic causes.

### 4.4 Decisive E2E fixtures

- Valid harness/confidential reads reach their actual readers; economic reads remain valid; wrong Work, malformed identity, revoked/cross-tenant principal and extra-envelope ambiguity fail correctly.
- Deliberate omission/context mutations fail manifest completeness; wait/check/native-only/human-only stay distinct.
- X1-origin X2 computation retains original source/rights/parent/deadline and reaches checker and owner; new-parent budget reset is rejected.
- A second eligible scientific family is selected from ordinary Work and reaches its actual consumer, chosen from a measured access gap rather than ceremonial registration.
- Lost provider response plus physical worker interruption, duplicate delivery and restart preserve original obligation/UNKNOWN and reconcile without blind replay.
- Claim/accept/use revocation races stop affected work without invalidating unrelated work.
- Mounted reload, Work/root/principal switch, token refresh, real logout/new sign-in, offline/reconnect and delayed old-principal response cannot resurrect private data or turn a candidate into verified success.
- A separate fresh locked installation emits and runs affected API/worker/browser paths. Donor dependencies and source-only probes are not reconstruction proof.

### 4.5 Gates and falsifier

All preregistered critical identity/authority/currentness/recovery cases pass, the supported positive owner flows run, generated catalogue completeness is independently tested, and final installed identity matches source/build/schema/browser receipts. Record every unrun surface.

Z0 fails if eligible supported capabilities remain inaccessible, native effects become unconstrained tools, transport creates false completion, budgets/UNKNOWN are lost, stale private state crosses principals or final installed positive paths remain unrun.

## 5. Z1 — Collective Semantic Work Compiler

### 5.1 Boundary

Compile **different independently material Works**, not identical requests, into one lawful exact computation and a change-maintenance programme. Preserve each Work's original result, acceptance, source rights, deadline, resources and effect identity.

X1/P7 already maintain dependencies; X5 reuses policies; Y5 can discover decompositions. Z1 must produce a jointly executable multi-Work object and its checked refinement. Ordinary caching, batching, SQL materialization or a DBSP wrapper alone does not establish a new boundary.

### 5.2 One output

```text
SharedWorkProgram
  envelope
  originalWorkAndProgrammeReferences
  originalInputAndOutputSemantics
  typedSharedComputationAndPerWorkProjection
  lawfulSharingPartitionAndDependencyGraph
  initialConstructionAndDeltaRules
  programmeEditAndRetractionRules
  exactRefinementAndCoverageCertificate
  perWorkCurrentnessAndResourceAccounting
  checkpointRecoveryAndInvalidationContract
```

No shared business/effect identity. Shared pure intermediate values do not authorize shared execution of separate commitments.

### 5.3 Required computation and mathematics

Let original pure supported computations be $f_i(D,\theta_i)$, with separate outputs and rights. Construct programme P and projections π_i so that:

$$
\forall i,\ \pi_i(P(D,\Theta))=f_i(D,\theta_i).
$$

For signed insertions/retractions δ and lawful programme changes η:

$$
\pi_i(U_P(P(D,\Theta),\delta,\eta))
=f_i^{\,\eta}(D\oplus\delta,\theta_i^{\,\eta}).
$$

The checker derives each original f_i, not only a producer's compressed DAG. It verifies type/unit/rounding semantics, multiplicities, empty sets, joins, ties, retractions, parameterized thresholds and every admitted rewrite/delta rule.

Discover common subexpressions and shared maintained views across **nonidentical** dependency graphs. Use hash-consing, algebraic rewrites, differential propagation, indexed views or self-adjusting execution only where exact and beneficial. A dirty dependent may require full recomputation; record and charge it.

Partition sharing by tenant, principal, permitted use, revision and security classification. No cross-tenant raw-data reuse or privately inferred feature leak. Shared work charges are allocated reproducibly, sum to the total, and never hide costs by charging no parent.

Programme edits include fresh objectives/constraints and withdrawn Works, not just new rows. A withdrawal releases only reversible computation; it does not erase S6 exposure or historical provenance.

### 5.4 First engineering profile

Finite typed pure relational/financial DAGs: exact integers/rationals, finite filters/projections, joins, unions with declared bag/set semantics, grouped sums, comparisons and deterministic bounded selections. Unsupported floating semantics, unbounded recursion and effects are refused.

Initial representation bounds: 2–1,000 distinct Works, at most 4,096 DAG nodes, 100,000 total source rows, 1,000,000 derived rows and 512-bit rational components. These are declared support/resource limits, not X/Y limits or novelty.

Derived values retain original units/currencies and canonical numerical rules. An overflow/size/deadline breach returns INCOMPLETE or an eligible direct fallback under the remaining parent, not silently approximate output.

### 5.5 Decisive fixtures

An independently specified portfolio has different covenant, liquidity, approval and readiness Works sharing lawful source transformations. Change a source record that affects only some Works; change an objective/threshold; retract a row; revoke one source; then withdraw one Work while another has an unresolved native obligation.

Every original result must equal independent direct recomputation and remain individually attributable. A stale unaffected-looking derived view, duplicated row, loss of a negative multiplicity, illicit shared private intermediate or merged effect identity fails.

Include independent, adversarial and **low-overlap** graphs. They expose discovery/maintenance overhead rather than guaranteeing a favorable sharing story.

### 5.6 Native integration and gates

The existing planner requests eligible candidate computation, the actual queue produces the SharedWorkProgram, the independent checker validates original projections, and native S3/S4 consumes each result under its own Work. S5 retains aggregate feasibility; S6 sees only the original independent actions.

Complete initial construction, updates, programme edits, retraction, revocation, crash/checkpoint recovery, negative checks, meters, installed packaging and mounted per-Work evidence. Require exact supported semantics and the §3.7 positive/critical gates.

Comparisons include direct full X/Y, its ordinary caches/indexes/DBSP/FIVM-compatible methods and an equipped joint compiler. Charge discovery, compile, storage, warm-up, every update, failures, checks and invalidation across the **registered actual reuse horizon**.

**Falsifier:** identical-query-only gains, fewer true assignments, hidden widened rights, missing original-history/source refinement, or equivalent equipped construction at comparable full cost. Z1 does not defeat an obligatory observation/approval/effect bottleneck.

## 6. Z2 — Coalition-Resilient Interaction Compiler

### 6.1 Boundary and preserved floor

Construct a lawful dynamic protocol that additionally excludes the declared **joint contingent coalition-deviation class**. Full Y3 already checks every unilateral contingent continuation and globally consistent beliefs; Z2 retains that entire floor.

This is not larger R5 allocation, a more accurate Y2 response model, an added participant count or “collusion-proof” branding. Y §6.3 expressly excludes coalition resistance. The exact stronger predicate below is the proposed new contract.

### 6.2 One output

```text
CoalitionResilientProtocol
  envelope
  originalModelOperatorProtocolFamilyAndTermsReferences
  nativeInteractionProtocolAndFullY3CertificateReference
  coalitionMembershipCommunicationAndTransferThreatProfile
  originalLawfulJointContingentPlanCoverage
  exactJointDeviationCertificatesOrCounterstrategies
  protocolConstructionTraceAndCheckedSearchBounds
  participationInformationResourceAndS6Bindings
  conceptScopeCurrentnessAndRecoveryContract
```

The protocol executes through existing participant-local Work and consents. The certificate is not an independent actor, consent or effect authority.

### 6.3 Exact first incentive concept

Coalitions commit their deviation **ex ante**, before the specified private-state draw; their plans can condition on every subsequently lawful local observation/message. This does not establish interim or recursively coalition-proof sequential equilibrium, arbitrary later coalition formation, or the stronger Abraham et al. k-resilience.

For every admitted original model m, coalition C and all original lawful pure joint contingent plans $d\in D_C(m)$, derive:

$$
g_{i,m}(d)=E_m[u_i(d,\sigma_{-C})]-E_m[u_i(\sigma)].
$$

Exclude every lawful correlated mixture p with all member gains nonnegative and at least one strictly positive:

$$
p\ge0,\quad {\bf1}^Tp=1,\quad
G_mp\ge0,\quad {\bf1}^TG_mp>0.
$$

The last expression is a feasibility device over normalized utilities, never aggregated cash or owner value. Preserve original scales/units, outside options, legal transfers and budgets.

The exact rational LP maximizes the last expression subject to the first three constraints. A no-profit dual witness supplies λ_i≥0 and ν≤0 satisfying:

$$
\forall d\in D_C(m):
\quad \nu\ge\sum_{i\in C}(1+\lambda_i)g_{i,m}(d).
$$

The independent checker reconstructs **complete lawful plans** and verifies every inequality. A profitable mixture is an exact counterexample. Approximate LP tolerances, sampled plans and heuristic best response do not certify absence.

### 6.4 Required construction

Compile original information sets, legal communication, state transitions, transfers, chance support and coalition coordination into local-history joint plans. Do not grant coalition members impossible pooled state; do not restrict an originally lawful communication/transfer channel to make the protocol pass.

Generate candidate information/commitment policies using the fixed admitted operator semantics and original protocol family. Use coalition counterstrategies to refine search. Every accepted candidate also passes the native full Y3 sequential-rationality/common-belief check, participation, nonanticipativity, mandatory disclosure, S5 resources and S6 terms/consent rules.

If some admitted model permits a profitable joint deviation, reject that robust claim. Do not average it away with favorable models. A narrower model/profile is a separately labelled result, not the original claim.

INFEASIBLE requires complete original candidate-family coverage and checked exclusion. Nonexistence is possible; it is not permission to invent penalties, change utilities, add legal powers or declare approximate stability exact.

### 6.5 First engineering profile and decisive fixtures

Two to four participants, two or more private-state values where supplied, at least three dates in dynamic positives, at most 64 original information sets, 128 candidate protocol policies and 4,096 lawful pure joint plans per coalition. Every nonempty coalition up to the declared k is covered; initial protected profiles use k=n. Exact rational chance/payoff data use at most 512-bit components.

These are finite model/representation limits, not Y3 ceilings. If the original family exceeds them, return unsupported/incomplete without pruning it.

Fixtures include:

- A protocol passing full unilateral checks but vulnerable to two participants coordinating an early report and a later action. A lawful alternative uses an **already authorized** evidence/commitment rule; the compiler, not a human patch, must construct/select it.
- Pure joint gain vectors (4,−2) and (−1,1); the 1/4–3/4 mixture gains (1/4,1/4). Pure-only testing must fail even when the protocol's own signal grid is coarse.
- A useful protocol with no such deviation under an independently reconstructed exact model, plus a genuinely infeasible protocol family.
- Forbidden private pooling, convenient off-path beliefs, missing common tremble sequence, a deleted legal side-payment plan, changed terms after consent and stale model revisions.

The protocol-building positive must be a dynamic institutional case, not only these two payoff vectors. The vectors test the checker, not completed business work.

### 6.6 Native integration, gates and falsifier

Reuse Y3's actual InteractionProtocol owner/certification path, supplied independently admitted models and fixed operators. The Z2 engine may use existing constructors but must add complete joint-deviation-driven construction and independent validation. Do not rebuild Y1/Y2 or depend on another Z engine.

An actual registered job produces, checks and persists the protocol; native S4 instantiates participant-local Work and prepares only authorized original effects. Loss/restart/revision preserves all identities and consents. Mount the exact incentive concept and excluded threat classes, not a generic “safe” badge.

Require full Y3 floors, exact joint predicate for every admitted model/coalition, §3.7 gates, real owner use and costed equipped comparison. MASE, rational secret sharing and ordinary game libraries remain equipment, not borrowed FINNOR guarantees.

**Falsifier:** any profitable permitted correlated joint continuation; weakened unilateral/belief floor; omitted transfer/communication; changed economic meaning; or an equivalent qualified equipped baseline at comparable complete cost. Actual counterparty compliance and economic gain remain separately measured.

---

## 7. Z3 — Infinite-Play Viability Compiler

### 7.1 Boundary

Compile a recurring original mandate into a strategy checked against **every supported infinite play**, not a longer replay, finite quotient, repeated replanning or another arbitrary-horizon theorem.

Credit full S4/R2, X1 standing responsibility, full Y3 and Y5. The new obligation is automatic mandate/game-to-winning-strategy computation with independent progress checking. An authoritative equivalent inherited obligation makes it REUSED.

### 7.2 One output and mathematical profile

```text
ViabilityStrategy
  envelope
  originalMandateObservationAndArenaReferences
  initialSetSafetyRecurrenceAndFairnessSemantics
  completeWinningRegionAndCheckedFixedPointEvidence
  finiteStrategyMemoryActionAndUpdateMaps
  independentAllPlayProgressAndNonvacuityChecks
  resourceRenewalUnknownAndS6Interpretation
  originalBehaviourRefinementAndRevisionBridge
```

Begin with a finite total turn-based perfect-information arena:

$$
G=(V_C\uplus V_E,E,I,S,\{A_i\},\{B_j\}).
$$

Require every initial state, every permitted environment choice and the actual deterministic strategy refinement:

$$
\forall v_0\in I,\ \forall\pi\in Plays(G,\sigma,v_0):
\quad {\bf G}S\ \land\
\left[(\bigwedge_i{\bf GF}A_i)\Rightarrow
(\bigwedge_j{\bf GF}B_j)\right].
$$

Safety is **outside** the fairness implication. Unfairness never authorizes unsafe action. Deadlocks have explicit losing/terminal semantics; empty successor sets cannot create vacuous wins.

### 7.3 Required synthesis and independent checks

Derive the arena from original admitted mandate/operation/resource meanings. Uncertain outcomes are environment moves, not favorable predictions. Controller actions use public present state only.

For U⊆V:

$$
CPre(U)=\{v\in V_C:\exists(v,w)\in E,\ w\in U\}
\cup\{v\in V_E:\forall(v,w)\in E,\ w\in U\}.
$$

Compute the safety kernel $W_S=\nu Z.(S\cap CPre(Z))$. Retain every environment successor. Within that kernel use a correctly qualified GR(1)/generalized-Büchi fixed-point algorithm and extract actual memory/progress actions. When there are no fairness assumptions use an unconditional assumption set V, not a convenient empty implication.

The independent checker:

1. reconstructs original transition coverage, labels and I;
2. checks complete least/greatest fixed-point iterants or an equivalent accepted certificate, not an arbitrary fixed point or one winning state;
3. checks legality/totality, memory initialization/update and all reachable environment branches;
4. constructs the reachable arena×strategy-memory graph;
5. for each B_j, removes B_j from that reachable graph and searches **all cyclic SCCs**, including non-bottom SCCs, that intersect every A_i;
6. rejects a fair progress-violating suffix, unsafe reachable state, stale binding or unmodelled action;
7. separately checks assumption satisfiability and controller-induced vacuity/non-conflict.

A rejecting strategy is not a proof of unrealizability. INFEASIBLE needs checked complete-region/dual evidence for the exact claimed property. If nonconflicting synthesis or its completeness is unresolved, return UNKNOWN rather than overstate a normal GR(1) result.

### 7.4 Honest infinite-time semantics

Initially support genuinely public fully observed state. Hidden persistent worlds/private values cannot be fed to a perfect-information solver. A future observation abstraction needs checked lifting and observation-uniform actions.

Arbitrary repetitions mean bounded **simultaneously** outstanding obligations, not unbounded queues/data. Recurrence of some completion does not discharge every request. Preserve each S6 identity, deadline and cancellation through explicit monitors. Wall-clock deadlines need time-advance/non-Zeno assumptions; GF alone gives no latency bound.

Fairness needs an independently admitted external basis. Forecasts do not prove lenders eventually agree, an authority eventually grants access, or UNKNOWN eventually resolves. A single global failed fairness premise can suspend all conditional liveness; show that exact scope.

Positive recurring cost with finite nonrenewing capital cannot support infinite completions. Any replenishment/renewal is an original admitted resource process, not a new grant. Actual execution always checks present finite resources/authority; the theorem cannot extend deadlines or erase liabilities.

### 7.5 First profile and decisive fixtures

At most 256 explicit states, eight outgoing choices per owner state, two environment justice sets and two recurring goals. Strategy memory and solver bounds are explicit proof/profile inputs. Eight-state subprofiles use independent exhaustive strategy/product references where finite-memory completeness is justified.

Start with controlled recurring internal reconciliation/readiness, not perpetual financing.

Fixtures: safe wait-only loop with no progress; locally attractive unserviceable cycle; permanent UNKNOWN locking capacity; nonrenewing budget exhaustion; controller-induced fairness failure; non-bottom fair bad SCC; two indistinguishable observations needing opposite actions; source/edge/goal/policy mutations; and a truly winning recurring controlled case surviving physical restart.

### 7.6 Native integration and gates

S4 consumes the checked strategy; S1/S3 retain model meaning; S5 retains actual resources; S6 retains authority and obligations; S8 admits; P7/native invalidation retracts stale claims. A refused proved action must be modelled or invalidate the guarantee. A replacement bridge preserves live exposure.

Require exact initial/all-play property, explicit nonvacuity, independently checked complete-region claims, §3.7 gates, real owner continuation and installed recovery. Libraries such as Omega are eligible equipment only after actual dependency/license/security checks.

**Falsifier:** a reachable supported unsafe/fair-progress-violating play, oracle-state action, invented fairness/renewal, obligation reset, equivalent inherited requirement or comparable equipped construction. Infinite mathematical correctness is not infinite accepted business work.

---

## 8. Z4 — Succinct Execution Integrity Compiler

### 8.1 Boundary

Compile an actual supported computation into compact independently checkable evidence that an untrusted executor performed that **specific relation-bound instance**. The verifier must not replay the full execution.

Y4 already compiles exact secure runtime functions; Y5 already provides uniform theorem and implementation correspondence. Z4 is neither another MPC protocol nor a theorem/hash/trace receipt. It adds a particular per-instance execution-integrity transformation.

### 8.2 One output

```text
ExecutionProofCapsule
  envelope
  originalTypedProgrammeAndExactRelationReferences
  compilerAndSemanticCorrespondenceCertificate
  publicStatementInputCommitmentsInitialAndFinalState
  actualExecutionLengthAndBoundaryBindings
  proofSystemParametersSetupAndSecurityProfile
  instanceProofAndVerifierReceipt
  privacyDisclosureCurrentnessAndFailureContract
```

Input truth, lawfulness, incentives, real-world effects and authority are explicitly outside the cryptographic relation unless independently supplied and bound.

### 8.3 Required computation

Derive a relation R_F from a runtime-revealed supported typed programme F. Bind exact code/constants/types/units/rounding, input commitments, initial/final state, execution length and semantics version. The admitted relation must not be selected by the untrusted prover.

For a step computation:

$$
R_F(s_t,x_t,s_{t+1})=1,\quad 0\le t<T.
$$

Produce a checked proof of the whole bound execution. A claimed computational soundness statement has the form:

$$
Pr[Verify(pp,statement,\Pi)=1\land statement\notin L(R_F)]
\le \varepsilon_{\text{profile}},
$$

under the **actually reviewed** assumptions and parameters. Do not invent a numerical security exponent or call a computational claim unconditional.

Use an appropriate proof-producing compiler/IVC/SNARK backend. Folding, recursion, compression and zero knowledge are distinct properties and must each be demonstrated if claimed. A digest chain, trusted executor signature or hash of an unverified trace does not pass.

Independently verify semantic correspondence and all bit/range/overflow/sign/division/rounding constraints. Field arithmetic alone cannot stand in for canonical financial arithmetic. A valid proof of the wrong relation is a failure.

### 8.4 First engineering profile and security limits

Runtime-defined bounded exact integer/fixed-scale kernels using original canonical units: up to 16 instructions per step, eight registers, signed 32-bit admitted inputs with explicitly checked widened 64-bit intermediates, and T in {1,2,4,8,16,64,256}. Support add/subtract, bounded multiplication, comparisons, selection and explicitly specified quotient/remainder where implemented exactly. Refuse unsupported floating/overflow/scale conversions.

These are first representation limits, not a claim to prove arbitrary TypeScript, a larger Y4 circuit, or a novelty from shorter verification alone. Protected cases include at least 40 **previously unseen function definitions/compositions**, revealed only after the general compiler is frozen, not merely fresh inputs to one built circuit.

Nova is a researched candidate, not an already adopted/qualified dependency: pin `01b1e2d662a847ed7029770ecbaf6687398899dd`, reported nova-snark 0.76/Rust≥1.88; verify the actual adopted cut, license, parameters and code. Test-only insecure random-tau setup is forbidden. No audit or security qualification is assumed.

No new trusted setup, signer, cryptographic ceremony or private input export is authorized. Prefer a properly reviewed transparent profile where appropriate. Missing production security qualification remains a separate blocked/unknown security state, not a fabricated proof of safety.

### 8.5 Decisive fixtures and cost crossover

For withheld programmes, execute real inputs and generate real proofs. Check against a separately implemented exact reference interpreter. Mutate output, input commitment, programme constant, length, intermediate range, final state, proof bytes, parameter identity and checker version; invalid statements must fail.

Include a valid field-level computation with incorrect integer overflow/rounding semantics. It must fail correspondence even if the proof backend accepts its field relation.

Measure proof size and actual verifier work across T while holding the relation/profile fixed. The intended succinct property is sublinear in full trace length, with all fixed parameters/disclosures counted. Record finite-range results and uncertainty, not an unproved universal asymptotic claim from a short plot.

For actual demand N verifiers:

$$
C_{\rm setup}+C_{\rm compile}+C_{\rm prove}
+N C_{\rm verify}+C_{\rm lifecycle}
\quad\text{versus}\quad
N C_{\rm eligible\ full\ checking}.
$$

Use the **registered real N/reuse horizon**, charge failed proofs and invalidation, and publish the crossover. With one verifier/prover-heavy work, this engine may lose. Do not hide that by pretending verification is free or by inventing repeated demand.

### 8.6 Native integration, gates and falsifier

The actual durable producer persists the capsule. An independent relation/correspondence and backend verifier runs before the native consumer uses the result. A replay/audit consumer verifies without full recomputation; S4/S5/S6 still perform their own currentness, feasibility and authority checks.

Keep proof output privacy within original Y4/source rights. Ordinary SNARK integrity is not automatically zero knowledge; unsupported confidentiality must be labelled and rejected where required.

Require real proof generation/verification, runtime-derived relation construction, exact correspondence, adverse mutation rejection, supported positive native use, interruption/retry identity, complete meters, installed native assets and §3.7 gates.

**Falsifier:** relation substitution, unconstrained witness/overflow, proofless trace, full verifier replay, new trust/privacy assumption, unqualified security claim, total-cost loss disguised by verifier-only timing or equivalent equipped baseline. A proof never authorizes a payment.

---

## 9. Z5 — Contextual Institution Linker

### 9.1 Boundary and nonduplication

Take **fixed independently admitted components**, their actual interfaces and an institutional property. Compute a checked compatible-context contract and a noncircular institutional composition/replacement certificate. Do not synthesize new component behavior to make the property true.

X2 already composes operations; X3 already verifies and maintains installed application invariants; Y5 already discovers decomposition proofs. The strengthened obligation is **universal assurance over an explicitly represented compatible-context language and contextual replacement**, with actual mutual assumptions discharged. Another integration test, conjunction of local assertions, safety theorem or new application is insufficient.

### 9.2 One output

```text
InstitutionCompositionCertificate
  envelope
  fixedComponentSemanticsAndImplementationBindings
  originalPortsSharedStateAndEnvironmentModel
  institutionalSafetyAndNonblockingProperty
  synthesizedCompatibleContextAutomata
  noncircularAssumptionGuaranteeAndCoverageEvidence
  contextualReplacementSimulationAndLiveStateBridge
  actualCompositionDischargeOrConcreteCounterexample
  currentnessResourceUnknownAndOwnerUseContract
```

The certificate is a capability/advisory input to the native owners and S8, not a new admission/enforcement owner.

### 9.3 Formal object and required computation

Represent admitted components as finite input/output labelled transition systems with declared legal initial states, ports, shared-state footprint, may/must transitions, quiescence, atomicity and failure semantics. Source hashes bind identity; separately qualified correspondence binds behavior. Hashing arbitrary code is not that correspondence.

Let $\parallel$ be the original admitted composition semantics, P the safety/nonblocking property, A the represented context language and R a contextual replacement relation. Establish:

$$
\forall E\in L(A):
\quad (C\parallel E)\models P
$$

and, when replacing C by C':

$$
\forall E\in L(A):
\quad Obs(C'\parallel E)\preceq_P Obs(C\parallel E).
$$

The exact preorder preserves required observations, authorization, resource/obligation identity and permitted quiescence/nonblocking; it is not an informal “seems equivalent” score.

Construct compatible assumption/context automata using finite language/game/interface operations and checked abstraction/refinement. If claiming weakest/maximally permissive context, prove both soundness and maximality under the exact supported preorder; otherwise label a sound subset, not the weakest contract.

For the actual components $C_1,\ldots,C_n$, discharge assumptions from **actual initial states and every original transition**, with an independently checked well-founded/inductive argument. “A assumes B is safe; B assumes A is safe” is not a proof. Empty accepted context languages or impossible mutual assumptions cannot certify an institution.

Check actual interface compatibility, legal quiescence and nonblocking. Conditional environmental cooperation is explicit. This phase does not claim Z3's all-play recurring liveness; it must not call existence of a completion path inevitable completion.

### 9.4 Coupling and code correspondence

Shared capital, locks, grants, source revisions, correlated worlds, calendars, private channels, delayed callbacks and UNKNOWN are real shared state, not independent local variables. The interface must retain their coupling, even when that makes it product-sized.

A component assumption cannot grant source access or a resource another owner has not supplied. All copies of a retained world refer to the same world; multiplying independent marginal possibilities cannot change the original joint model.

Initial supported correspondence can use exact bounded typed transition kernels and independently checked finite translation/refinement. General database/TypeScript behavior is not proved by an abstract LTS. Native integration must expose its exact qualified transition contract and implementation revision; unsupported code correspondence remains partial/unknown and cannot carry an actual broad institution claim.

Replacement binds actual live states, reservations and unresolved S6 obligations. An unreachable clean initial state cannot stand in for a running institution. If a safe bridge is absent, stop/retain the old component without erasing liabilities.

### 9.5 First profile and decisive fixtures

Two to sixteen finite components, at most 32 local states each, sixteen port labels, eight explicitly coupled finite shared cells and a declared context automaton bound of 4,096 states. Atomicity and may/must semantics are input contracts, not assumptions supplied by implementation convenience.

Use independently exhaustively checkable small products for reference, then larger modular cases whose interface/certificate sizes and complete costs are reported. Interface blow-up is a result, not permission to drop dependencies.

Decisive controlled institution: original S4 proposal, S5 reservation and S6 dispatch/reconciliation contracts are individually valid. A terms change races a reservation and delayed provider callback. Naive local assurances permit stale dispatch, duplicate exposure or circular wait. The linker must produce a real counterexample for that composition and accept a genuinely compatible **existing** component/revision choice with a checked live-state bridge.

Also test hidden common capital, inconsistent world IDs, mismatched ports, empty/circular assumptions, legal UNKNOWN quiescence, a nonblocking violation, false source binding and a replacement that loses an old obligation.

The accepted positive may choose among supplied already admitted components. Constructing a new workflow/controller belongs to other owners and does not pass Z5's fixed-component boundary.

### 9.6 Native integration, gates and falsifier

An actual registered job derives/checks the certificate. Native S4/S8 integration consumes only its declared supported contract, while S5/S6 continue independent present feasibility/authority. The mounted Work shows compatible context, exact conditionality, actual component revisions and a counterexample or bridge.

Require independent context/simulation checks, actual assumption discharge, original shared-state preservation, nonempty compatibility, supported implementation correspondence, §3.7 gates, real owner consumption, invalidation and physical recovery.

Compare against full X/Y with standard model checking, Pacti/OCRA-compatible methods and ordinary Y5 composition proofs. Charge contract extraction, product exploration, abstraction refinement, checking, integration, replacement and every invalidation. Compactness must be demonstrated, not assumed.

**Falsifier:** circular assumptions, hidden coupling, empty/vacuous contexts, changed component behavior, unknown correspondence labelled proven, lost live obligations, an equivalent inherited obligation or comparable equipped construction. Safety-only library results are not whole-code/liveness guarantees.

---

## 10. Integrated qualification and the defensible 100× route

### 10.1 Why a route is not a forecast

Potential headroom comes from changing computational units: jointly maintained distinct Works, joint incentive qualification, all-play recurring strategies, compact instance verification and contextual composition. None supplies an automatic multiplier.

Before scaling, measure full X/Y accepted-work demand by resource and by work class. Map each new engine to a measured binding term or a formerly unaccepted **same-criterion** business result. If the baseline can cheaply construct the same method, include it.

For a serial unchanged binding share f, even making all other work free caps the resource gain at 1/f. A 2% unchanged share caps it at 50×. In a steady-state binding resource j, a necessary condition is:

$$
d_{Z,j}\le d_{XY,j}/100,
$$

including new overhead. Use actual concurrency, pipelines and queues elsewhere; latency is not throughput. Mandatory source observations, human approvals, counterparty participation, provider dispatch and unresolved exposure may make 100× impossible. Publish that result rather than buy unmatched resources or lower acceptance.

### 10.2 Registered campaign and transfer

Preserve inherited reach: 90 operational days, 100 companies/deals, ten system types, 1,000 concurrent missions, 10,000 interacting decisions, sixteen firms, eight strategic counterparties, four resource pools and K={1,2,4,8,16,64,256,1000}, using the actual frozen generator coordinates.

Run a second independently administered institution/cohort for transfer without copied private facts, grants, hidden models or oracle answers. Replay, controlled services and real field activity remain distinct evidence classes.

At least thirty independent institution/period blocks support simultaneous 95% inference, increased by independent power analysis. Thousands of correlated steps are not independent blocks.

### 10.3 Comparison actors and bounds

Mandatory B_XY_release includes all S/P/M/R/V/X/Y methods, learned assets, code generation, qualified standard scientific/cryptographic libraries, native specialists, strongest fallback and equivalent Z0 repairs. Give all actors the same lawful new method-discovery/construction rights and preparation resources.

Freeze actor versions, model access, eligible libraries, observations/data, rights, capital, risk, contact exposure, horizon, resources and actual prices on disjoint calibration data. A competitor is included only when an appropriately configured version actually runs; inaccessible is NOT_RUN, not defeated.

Register offered loads before protected exposure. Establish candidate lower capacity from qualifying cells. Comparator upper capacity includes unresolved potentially qualifying cells and all untested possibly qualifying loads; a failed high-load trial does not rule out every intermediate load. Do not extrapolate outside the qualified grid.

Measure claimed-completion correctness **before** filtering accepted results into the work numerator. Keep overload, rescue, measurement errors, wrong-build/session runs and resource breaches.

### 10.4 Floors retained

- Correctness/goal completion ≥95% under the original estimator.
- Normalized regret or explicitly labelled reference shortfall ≤0.05.
- Calibration coverage error ≤0.02 at 50/80/90/95%; mean 90% interval width ≤0.5 of the frozen range.
- Zero **observed** authority/tenant/audit/verification violations in the tested supported domain, not a universal zero-risk claim.
- Original episode: $100 compute/data/integration; 900 human seconds; 16 GiB aggregate simultaneous peak; 30-second initial response; 3,600-second wall.
- Preparation: 80 human hours and $10,000 per admitted actor.
- Hard cash, capital, liability, units, timing, rights, original parent and all inherited X/Y floors remain.

Children, retries, checking, fallbacks, observer timeouts and new chats do not reset the parent. Register multiweek construction/learning/proof/lifecycle aggregate envelopes separately.

### 10.5 Five ablations, no product of ratios

| Disabled engine, with all other eligible equipment retained | Specific evidence that must disappear or materially change |
|---|---|
| Z1 | Checked joint change-maintained execution of different material Works |
| Z2 | Exact declared joint contingent coalition-resistance construction/certificate |
| Z3 | Automatic all-play winning/recurring-progress strategy assurance |
| Z4 | Compact relation-bound proof of actual untrusted execution |
| Z5 | Noncircular compatible-context/replacement assurance for fixed components |

Compare full candidate with all five disabled against B_XY_release. A redundant engine or successful baseline reconstruction is evidence against the claimed increment. Z0 is available to both sides; broken baseline UI cannot manufacture superiority.

All five increment gates and all original floors must pass, then the **single** §1.2 100× endpoint must pass. Component savings cannot be multiplied.

### 10.6 Economic headroom and financial gates

Register a material signed business-decision-payoff improvement margin excluding clerical savings, fixed independently before exposure. Require its simultaneous lower bound to exceed that margin under matched capital/rights/risk/resources/horizon.

Preserve the $100 million additional **net attributable owner-value** objective and original principal economic protocol, including applicable named comparator gates. S7 independently accounts for all unsuccessful programmes, human overrides, attribution/identification/valuation uncertainty, missing outcomes and full costs.

Track H0 observed/settled state, H1 provisional outcomes and H2 at the 36-month mature horizon. A new financial 100× over full X/Y additionally requires:

$$
L_{Z,\mathrm{net\ owner\ value}}\ge
100\max_b U_{b,\mathrm{net\ owner\ value}},\qquad
L_{XY,\mathrm{net\ owner\ value}}\ge\epsilon_{\mathrm{financial},XY}>0.
$$

Capital raised, AUM, transaction value, leverage, modeled surplus, simulated cash, predicted returns and market appreciation do not establish additional attributable wealth. Operational and financial 100× have separate statuses. Neither opportunity supply nor economic headroom is assumed.

### 10.7 Complete status reporting

A completed implementation with less than 100× is partial. Missing qualified baseline, protected oracle/custody, complete meters, registered material epsilons or eligible backlog is BLOCKED for comparison. Missing internal engineering is INCOMPLETE. Negative/infeasible results are valuable correctness evidence, not positive throughput.

---

## 11. Infrastructure, product behavior and autonomous execution

### 11.1 One product graph, no new truth system

```text
canonical Work / mandate
 → Z0 coherent native intake and observations
 → eligible Z1 / Z2 / Z3 / Z4 / Z5 computations (independently usable)
 → independently checked, current artifacts on existing persistence
 → actual S3 / S4 consumers
 → shared S5 resources
 → existing S6 authority / obligations / observations
 → S7 outcomes and costs
 → existing S8 admission and native invalidation
 → original mounted Work
```

This is not a new workflow engine. New outputs may improve one another later only through actual delivered/admitted contracts, not mandatory serial dependencies.

### 11.2 Complete meters

Retain actual provider/model/checkpoint and reported token/cache/reasoning usage/pricing; CPU/GPU time; aggregate peak memory; storage/network; data/experiments; compile/search/rejected candidates; setup/proving/checking; integration/deployment/reconstruction; failure/recovery/invalidation; all human preparation/supervision/rescue; and effects/reservations/unresolved exposure.

Allocate shared costs reproducibly without double counting or dropping them. Unknown material costs are unknown, never zero. Amortize only over registered actual eligible demand and retention horizon. Disclose general engineering expenditure separately in lifecycle analysis; do not invent historical baseline development costs.

The current development host has about 8 GiB physical RAM. That is not a 16 GiB qualification receipt. Bound local concurrent processes; run heavy installed tests serially unless the bounded concurrency case itself is under test. No GPU/cluster/production credential is assumed.

### 11.3 Isolation, dependencies and reproducibility

Generated programmes, semantic models, proof search and checker inputs are untrusted. Bound CPU, memory, wall time, filesystem/network and credentials. Native scientific jobs use the existing queue/fences/cancellation, not arbitrary execution with platform credentials.

Pin actual dependencies/lockfiles/toolchains and native assets. Root requires Node 22.x; observed Node 24 is not compliance. Borrowed node_modules, hidden alternates, missing local Git objects or an undeclared Rust/Python binary do not pass a cold reconstruction.

Read installed Next.js documentation before relevant edits. Check transitive licenses/native build assumptions. No source/library is adopted because a paper mentions it.

AWS follows current selected project/Region and AGENTS.md. No provisioning, paid-plan change, second Region or Lambda@Edge is authorized. A missing cloud grant blocks that external activity, not locally achievable engineering.

### 11.4 User-facing behavior

The current Work/focus surface shows:

- the original mandate/Work, current terms and remaining resources;
- which eligible computation is requested and why;
- sourced fact, assertion, model-relative claim, exact proof or UNKNOWN basis;
- original input/model/code/checker revisions and currentness;
- actual producer/checker/consumer state and independent observation;
- counterexamples, unsupported profile, incomplete search and actionable gaps;
- original S6 obligation/UNKNOWN/reconciliation, never a fabricated success;
- cost/security/qualification/100× states separately.

Use mounted controls, keyboard/semantic accessibility where relevant and actual reload/session switching. Do not replace engineering with dashboards or hide a missing owner behind a progress label.

### 11.5 Required roles and exact model policy

One PRD/coordinator chat owns architecture, shared contracts, integration, progress/conflict resolution and independent qualification coordination. One distinct prompt-engineering chat owns the exact reusable blueprint and six complete phase prompts. Six execution chats each require a **different actually provisioned dedicated Droid Computer**, branch and worktree.

Every Droid/LLM role, including research, orchestrator, worker, validator and any allowed subagent, is exactly **`gpt-6.1-sol`, reasoning `max`**. Pin and inspect actual settings/receipts. No automatic routing, alternate model, silent fallback or default light agent. A requested flag alone is not a runtime attestation.

Independent mathematical/protected authority does not come from model diversity. Non-LLM deterministic scientific libraries are not alternate chat models.

### 11.6 Mandatory three-slot schedule

After the common product lock **and complete execution pack** exist, request Z0+Z1+Z2 as one concurrent initial launch group. Confirm actual Computer/session/model start receipts. Three requests are not three active phases.

At most three implementation slots are active. On a valid lightweight scheduling handoff, immediately refill in order Z3, then Z4, then Z5. Research and certification are not substitute execution slots.

The lightweight check verifies exact source/base/tree/product lock, ownership/changed-file manifest, immutable finite verification dispositions/failures, reconstruction inputs, integration requests, remaining budgets/obligations and next-context delivery. It is **not** deep certification, code completion by assertion, protected admission or a reason to wait for whole-programme qualification.

Keep deep source scrutiny, installed user testing, protected mathematical/security/financial qualification and comparative certification separate. Do not serialize six phases to wait for them. If a lane exits incomplete or actual Computers/permissions are absent, record the true underfill/blocker; never call it complete or active to satisfy the number.

### 11.7 Complete prompts and delivery

Each execution seed contains the **full locked PRD**, actual common contracts/source snapshot, its complete five-section prompt, relevant earlier complete prompts, actual delivered peer handoffs and all applicable pinned engineering skill texts. It is not a memory-summary handoff.

Use exactly `/goal` and these headings:

1. OUTCOME AND COMPLETION PREDICATE
2. VERIFIED STARTING POINT AND INTEGRATION BOUNDARIES
3. IMPLEMENTATION FREEDOM AND REQUIRED ENGINEERING METHODS
4. ACCEPTANCE EVIDENCE
5. EXECUTION CONTROL, FINISH DISCIPLINE AND HANDOFF

Section 4 includes A–J: mechanism; adversarial semantics; uncertainty; information; currentness; authenticated durable positive owner flow; physical interruption/recovery with remaining-budget fallback; mounted Work; final separately installed reconstruction; equipped comparison/ablation.

Every final handoff uses exactly the seven labels **IMPLEMENTED / CHANGED / EVIDENCE / COMPLETION GATE / REMAINING LIMITATIONS / FRONTIER DELTA / NEXT DEPENDENCIES** and reports truthful states.

Seeds are complete fallback artifacts when supported remote creation/messaging is unavailable. Record REQUESTED, DELIVERED_WITH_RECEIPT, ACKNOWLEDGED_READ, NOT_DELIVERED or DELIVERY_UNKNOWN. File existence here never proves another chat received it.

### 11.8 Layered lock and transport

Avoid self-referential digests:

1. source snapshots/read audit;
2. immutable PRD, source index, common contracts and scope decision → product lock;
3. full blueprint, six prompt bodies, assembled seeds, controller/role/reconstruction instructions → execution-pack lock;
4. each launch → immutable current-source/actual-received-peer context snapshot.

Later reality does not rewrite earlier evidence. Verify every delivery's hashes and source identity. A local bundle is not proof it exists on a dedicated Computer. Missing transport remains NOT_DELIVERED.

### 11.9 Available permission and actual blockers

Task-owned reversible source work, finite disposable local verification, local commits and programme-requested context delivery are within scope. Do not push, open PRs, merge, deploy to production, grant access, create signers, contact customers/counterparties or make real financial commitments.

The six already staged desktop Computer creation proposals require the user's renderer-owned apply action. Do not bypass them with shell/API provisioning. Source/session metadata reads are not launch receipts. Two attempted session-message writes returned HTTP 400; their delivery is NOT_CONFIRMED, not successful.

If remote orchestration is unavailable, finish full versioned chat seeds and transport/handoff artifacts and continue safe engineering within actual permissions. Explicitly report that local preparation is **not** six dedicated executions or the mandatory three-active-phase result.

### 11.10 Finish discipline

Follow repository instructions and pinned engineering skills. Write failure models first, run narrow relevant E2E/type/lint/build checks, repair failures, then broaden only for a reason. Preserve user/untracked work and original failures.

No outward action is inferred from a tool being connected. No unauthorized commit identity changes, history rewrite or hook bypass. Any authorized local commit includes the required Factory coauthor. A clean status or document validation is not product evidence.

---

## 12. Primary sources, interpretation and acceptance responsibility

### 12.1 Governing supplied sources

`sources/source-index.json` binds the exact retained request, X fragments/reconstruction, Y PRD and complete Y2/Y3 blueprints. `research/supplied-source-reading-audit.md` records complete reading and reconstruction limits.

Current Z instructions override obsolete source-specific phase goals, model choices and publication permissions. Quoted Y2/Y3 prompts are execution-structure sources, not instructions to start those old missions.

### 12.2 Primary research used and what it does not prove

| Source | Concrete use | Evidence ceiling |
|---|---|---|
| DBSP VLDB paper, <https://docs.feldera.com/vldb23.pdf> | Differential/incremental semantics and construction cost | Not a FINNOR multi-Work or 100× result |
| Self-adjusting computation, <https://arxiv.org/abs/1106.0478> | Change propagation and overhead tradeoffs | No universal cheap update or new contract by itself |
| FIVM pinned artifact `e55304d97ae06e1ee4aea3dd7b8d4c422d361667` | Existing maintained-view comparator, limited supported queries | No general finance/recursive runtime guarantee |
| Liu/Farina/Ozdaglar 2026, <https://arxiv.org/html/2604.28186v1> | Coalition objective distinctions, hardness and algorithm limits | MASE/approximation is not Z2's exact dynamic predicate |
| Abraham et al. PODC 2006, <https://www.cs.cornell.edu/home/halpern/papers/podc06.pdf> | Local strategies, coalition notions, synchrony/channel/utility premises | No blanket strong resilience, mediator removal or MPC fairness for FINNOR |
| Hausmann/Lehaut/Piterman, <https://arxiv.org/html/2305.02793v2> | Winning regions, strategies and safety/liveness complexity | General synthesis can be hard; overall implementation was future work |
| Omega `de9743df2df90c1a377c710183e9ace7f179ac10` | Existing GR(1) solver/strategy extraction reference | Not an installed/adopted or independently certified FINNOR backend |
| Nova `01b1e2d662a847ed7029770ecbaf6687398899dd` | Concrete IVC/proof implementation candidate | No assumed audit, security setup, compiler correspondence or FINNOR cost win |
| Pacti/OCRA and Ni/Wang ECOOP 2026 sources in frontier register | Existing contract/compositional verification methods | Safety/abstract-model results are not full-code liveness or native admission |
| *Fixing the Fixpoint*, <https://arxiv.org/html/2610.00530v1> | Warning that internal convergence/fixpoint shortcuts can be unsound | Not a verified replacement algorithm |

The detailed register, pinned artifacts, inspected sections, limits and rejected candidates are in `research/frontier-adversarial-report.md`, `research/frontier-followup-report.md` and `research/scope-selection-decision.md`. Do not upgrade a search snippet, abstract, rendered fetch or partially read proof to a complete audit. No dependency adoption/benchmark is implied by this table.

Slugs' inspected Python2/academic-only bundled dddmp issues forbid casual adoption of that bundle. General fairness with n−1 active corruption and revoked-influence deletion are rejected as Z scopes for impossibility/duplication reasons, not hidden omissions.

### 12.3 Engineering references

Use the exact pinned twelve leaf skill texts registered by the prompt-engineering chat: Ponytail `2961b52fc1fe6a5a87c7a959a79512e5c2f3ffe5`; pstack `1f0f9c3bac03c4297f6fc8f7644fa412eda57085`; Cursor plugins `ccb5507cec1546dc88135c1139c811e6c59115ba`; OpenClaw `b90a911280addee572e1f1ed9d02c9226adf64a7`.

Required applicable methods include scope/architecture, domain modelling, failure-first proof/verification, explanation of numerical claims, benchmarking, adversarial review and test audit. Read actual bodies; names/URLs are not completion. Repository testing/security/authority constraints override a skill's incompatible defaults. Missing project-specific verification skill remains explicit until created from the actual locked contract and reviewed.

### 12.4 Acceptance responsibility and present state

The coordinator accepts architecture/ownership and scheduling records. Independent governed checkers/oracles accept their exact mathematical/protected claims. Native S8 accepts capabilities; native S4/S5/S6 retain use/feasibility/effect authority; S7 accepts attribution/economics. Neither coordinator prose nor a producer can collapse those roles.

At this requirements cut there are **zero claimed completed Z engines, zero protected Z admissions, zero qualified strict equipped-baseline witnesses and zero measured 100× results**. The locked programme supplies concrete computations, finite first domains, independent falsifiers and a path to test the target. If the experiments reject it, retain UNPASSED and the evidence.
